[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 2/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes with cost 145429 (area 108.7, delay 1338ps). Now let me create an optimized architecture. My key idea: instead of doing a full 22-bit leading-zero count + 22-bit barrel shift after the multiply, I'll **normalize subnormal inputs before multiplication** (10-bit LZC + small shift per operand). Then the product is always normalized (leading 1 at bit 20 or 21), so normalization is just a 2:1 mux. Only the subnormal-result path needs a small right shifter.


  -> create_file(filename='v2_prenorm.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier, subnormals pre-normalized before multiply.\n// Architecture: 10-bit LZC + small left shift normalize subnormal operands so the\n// 11x11 significand product is always normalized (leading one at bit 21 or 20).\n// Normal-result normalization is then a 2:1 mux; only the subnormal-result path\n// needs a small right shifter (flat 11:1 mux, pure wiring per lane).\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 9; i >= 0; i = i - 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 9; i >= 0; i = i - 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (hidden one restored for subnormals) ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective biased exponents: normal e, subnormal -lz ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n\n  // ---- significand product (always in [1,4): leading one at bit 21 or 20) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  // ---- biased exponent of the exact product, as if normal ----\n  wire signed [6:0] er = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15 + {6'd0, p21};\n\n  // ---- mantissa fields of the exact product ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n\n  // ---- normal-path rounding (RTNE) ----\n  wire        rup    = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_r = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry  = frac_r[10];\n\n  wire signed [6:0] exp_n  = er + {6'd0, carry};\n  wire              under  = (er <= 7'sd0);              // subnormal-result path\n  wire              over   = ~under & (exp_n >= 7'sd31); // overflow -> inf\n\n  // ---- subnormal-result path: shift 1.frac onto the subnormal grid ----\n  wire [10:0] S      = p21 ? prod[21:11] : prod[20:10];\n  wire [5:0]  sh_raw = 6'd1 - er[5:0];                   // 1..34\n  wire [3:0]  sh     = (sh_raw > 6'd11) ? 4'd12 : sh_raw[3:0];\n\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (sh)\n      4'd1:  begin f_tr = S[10:1];  g_s = S[0];  st_s = 1'b0;      end\n      4'd2:  begin f_tr = S[10:2];  g_s = S[1];  st_s = S[0];      end\n      4'd3:  begin f_tr = S[10:3];  g_s = S[2];  st_s = |S[1:0];   end\n      4'd4:  begin f_tr = S[10:4];  g_s = S[3];  st_s = |S[2:0];   end\n      4'd5:  begin f_tr = S[10:5];  g_s = S[4];  st_s = |S[3:0];   end\n      4'd6:  begin f_tr = S[10:6];  g_s = S[5];  st_s = |S[4:0];   end\n      4'd7:  begin f_tr = S[10:7];  g_s = S[6];  st_s = |S[5:0];   end\n      4'd8:  begin f_tr = S[10:8];  g_s = S[7];  st_s = |S[6:0];   end\n      4'd9:  begin f_tr = S[10:9];  g_s = S[8];  st_s = |S[7:0];   end\n      4'd10: begin f_tr = S[10];    g_s = S[9];  st_s = |S[8:0];   end\n      4'd11: begin f_tr = 10'd0;    g_s = S[10]; st_s = |S[9:0];   end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;  g_s = 1'b0;  st_s = 1'b0;      end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | s_n | f_tr[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};\n  wire        carry_s = frac_s[10];   // rounded up into the smallest normal\n  wire        sub_zero = ~carry_s & (frac_s[9:0] == 10'd0);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = any_nan;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (any_inf | over);\n  wire is_zero = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (any_zero | (under & sub_zero));\n  wire is_sub  = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & under & ~sub_zero;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_zero             ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub              ? {4'd0, carry_s} :\n                                            exp_n[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      is_sub  ? (carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_s[9:0]) :\n                                frac_r[9:0];\n  wire sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='v2_prenorm.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier, subnormals pre-normalized before multiply.\n// Architecture: 10-bit LZC + small left shift normalize subnormal operands so the\n// 11x11 significand product is always normalized (leading one at bit 21 or 20).\n// Normal-result normalization is then a 2:1 mux; the subnormal-result path is a\n// small right shifter on the product.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  // ---- classify ----\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions (lz = 9 - MSB index) ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands (hidden one restored for subnormals) ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective biased exponents: normal e, subnormal -lz ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n\n  // ---- significand product (always in [2^20, 2^22)) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  // ---- biased exponent of the exact product, as if normal ----\n  wire signed [6:0] er = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15 + {6'd0, p21};\n\n  // ---- mantissa fields of the exact product ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n\n  // ---- normal-path rounding (RTNE) ----\n  wire        rup    = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_r = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry  = frac_r[10];\n\n  wire signed [6:0] exp_n = er + {6'd0, carry};\n  wire              under = (er <= 7'sd0);                // subnormal-result path\n  wire              over  = ~under & (exp_n >= 7'sd31);   // overflow -> inf\n\n  // ---- subnormal-result path: shift exact product onto the subnormal grid ----\n  // frac16_exact = prod >> t, t = 11 + p21 - er ; t in [11, 44], >=23 gives 0\n  wire [5:0] t = 6'd11 + {5'd0, p21} - er[5:0];\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (t)\n      6'd11: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end\n      6'd12: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end\n      6'd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end\n      6'd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end\n      6'd15: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end\n      6'd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end\n      6'd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end\n      6'd18: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end\n      6'd19: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end\n      6'd20: begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end\n      6'd21: begin f_tr = {9'd0, prod[21]};    g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end\n      6'd22: begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;             g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | f_tr[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};\n  wire        carry_s = frac_s[10];   // rounded up into the smallest normal\n  wire        sub_zero = ~carry_s & (frac_s[9:0] == 10'd0);\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire is_nan  = any_nan;\n  wire is_inf  = ~is_nan & (any_inf | over);\n  wire is_zero = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (any_zero | (under & sub_zero));\n  wire is_sub  = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & under & ~sub_zero;\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_zero             ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub              ? {4'd0, carry_s} :\n                                            exp_n[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan  ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      is_sub  ? (carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_s[9:0]) :\n                                frac_r[9:0];\n  wire sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='v3_unified.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v3: subnormal inputs pre-normalized (10-bit LZC + small left shift), so the\n// 11x11 significand product is always normalized. Both rounding paths share one\n// right shifter; the exponent path is pure 5-bit saturated arithmetic.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = 10 + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands; effective exponents in 6-bit signed ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n\n  // ---- significand product: always normalized ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire [6:0] er = (Ea + Eb - 7'sd15 + {6'd0, p21}) & 7'h7F;   // biased exp, as-if-normal\n\n  // ---- wide sticky: any bit of prod strictly below bit (t-1) ----\n  // t in [1,16] only ever needed; stick[k] = |prod[k-1:0]\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  assign below[0] = prod[0];\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 1; k < 22; k = k + 1) assign below[k] = below[k-1] | prod[k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire [15:0] stick = {below[15], below[14], below[13], below[12],\n                       below[11], below[10], below[9],  below[8],\n                       below[7],  below[6],  below[5],  below[4],\n                       below[3],  below[2],  below[1],  1'b0};\n\n  // ---- single right shift t = 11 + p21 - er, clamped to [0, 15] ----\n  wire [6:0] t_raw = 7'd11 + {6'd0, p21} - er;   // er<28 for non-overflow results\n  wire       sub_path = t_raw[6];                // er <= 0 : subnormal result\n  wire       ovf_path = ~sub_path & t_raw[5];    // er >= 32: overflow (raw t<0)\n  wire       big      = |t_raw[4];\n  wire [3:0] tc       = big ? [ASSISTANT]
I found two bugs in v3 (a loop typo and incorrect sticky indexing). I'll rewrite it using a cleaner, more unified derivation: take V = prod[21:9] (13 bits), and do a single right shift s, where the mantissa/guard/sticky are all obtained from a single index calculation.


  -> create_file(filename='v3_unified.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v3: subnormal inputs pre-normalized (10-bit LZC + small left shift), so the\n// 11x11 significand product is always normalized. One shared right shifter\n// (13-bit window V = prod[21:9]) handles both normal mantissa extraction and\n// subnormal-result alignment; one shared rounding incrementer; 7-bit exponent\n// arithmetic only.\n//\n// Derivation: value(prod) = prod * 2^(Ea+Eb-50); er = Ea+Eb-15+p21 is the\n// biased exponent as-if-normal. With V = prod[21:9] and\n//   s = subnormal-path ? (2+p21-er) : (1+p21)\n// we have mant = (V>>s)[9:0], guard = V[s-1], sticky = |V[s-2:0] | |prod[8:0].\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands; effective exponents (7-bit signed) ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n\n  // ---- significand product: always normalized (leading one at bit 21/20) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire signed [6:0] er = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15 + {6'd0, p21};\n  wire              sub_path = (er <= 7'sd0);\n\n  // ---- unified right shift on the 13-bit window V = prod[21:9] ----\n  wire signed [6:0] s_sub = 7'sd2 + {6'd0, p21} - er;      // >= 2 on subnormal path\n  wire [6:0]        s     = sub_path ? s_sub : (7'd1 + {6'd0, p21});\n  wire              big   = (s > 7'd14);\n  wire [3:0]        sc    = big ? 4'd14 : s[3:0];\n\n  wire [15:0] V16 = {3'd0, prod[21:9]};\n  wire [9:0]  mant  = V16[9:0] >> sc;\n  wire        guard = (sc == 4'd0) ? 1'b0 : V16[sc - 4'd1];\n\n  // sticky: |V[sc-2:0] | |prod[8:0], prefix OR over V\n  wire [12:0] belowV;\n  assign belowV[0] = prod[9];\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 1; k < 13; k = k + 1) assign belowV[k] = belowV[k-1] | prod[9+k];\n  endgenerate\n  wire below8 = |prod[8:0];\n  wire sticky = below8 | ((sc >= 4'd2) ? belowV[(sc >= 4'd14) ? 4'd12 : sc - 4'd2] : 1'b0);\n\n  // ---- rounding (RTNE), single shared incrementer ----\n  wire        rup      = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_rnd = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry    = frac_rnd[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_out = frac_rnd[9:0];\n\n  // ---- result exponent ----\n  wire [4:0] er5   = er[4:0];\n  wire       ovf   = ~sub_path & ((er5 + {4'd0, carry}) >= 5'd31);\n  wire [4:0] e_y   = ovf ? 5'd31 : (sub_path ? {4'd0, carry} : (er5 + {4'd0, carry}));\n\n  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~any_inf & (any_zero | ((frac_out == 10'd0) & ~carry));\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (any_nan | any_inf) ? 5'd31 : res_zero ? 5'd0 : e_y;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 : (any_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 : frac_out;\n  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.
// v3: subnormal inputs pre-normalized (10-bit LZC + small left shift), so the
// 11x11 significand product is always normalized. One shared right shifter
// (13-bit window V = prod[21:9]) handles both normal mantissa extraction and
// subnormal-result alignment; one shared rounding incrementer; 7-bit exponent
// arithmetic only.
//
// Derivation: value(prod) = prod * 2^(Ea+Eb-50); er = Ea+Eb-15+p21 is the
// biased exponent as-if-normal. With V = prod[21:9] and
//   s = subnormal-path ? (2+p21-er) : (1+p21)
// we have mant = (V>>s)[9:0], guard = V[s-1], sticky = |V[s-2:0] | |prod[8:0].
module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  // ---- unpack & classify ----
  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];
  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];

  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);
  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;
  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);
  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;
  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;
  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;

  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;

  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----
  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;
  integer i;
  always @* begin
    lza = 4'd9;
    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];
  end
  always @* begin
    lzb = 4'd9;
    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];
  end

  // ---- normalized significands; effective exponents (7-bit signed) ----
  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);
  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);
  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};
  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};

  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});
  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});

  // ---- significand product: always normalized (leading one at bit 21/20) ----
  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;
  wire        p21  = prod[21];

  wire signed [6:0] er = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15 + {6'd0, p21};
  wire              sub_path = (er <= 7'sd0);

  // ---- unified right shift on the 13-bit window V = prod[21:9] ----
  wire signed [6:0] s_sub = 7'sd2 + {6'd0, p21} - er;      // >= 2 on subnormal path
  wire [6:0]        s     = sub_path ? s_sub : (7'd1 + {6'd0, p21});
  wire              big   = (s > 7'd14);
  wire [3:0]        sc    = big ? 4'd14 : s[3:0];

  wire [15:0] V16    = {3'd0, prod[21:9]};
  wire [15:0] V_sh   = V16 >> sc;
  wire [9:0]  mant   = V_sh[9:0];
  wire        guard = (sc == 4'd0) ? 1'b0 : V16[sc - 4'd1];

  // sticky: |V[sc-2:0] | |prod[8:0], prefix OR over V
  wire [12:0] belowV;
  assign belowV[0] = prod[9];
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 1; k < 13; k = k + 1) assign belowV[k] = belowV[k-1] | prod[9+k];
  endgenerate
  wire below8 = |prod[8:0];
  wire sticky = below8 | ((sc >= 4'd2) ? belowV[(sc >= 4'd14) ? 4'd12 : sc - 4'd2] : 1'b0);

  // ---- rounding (RTNE), single shared incrementer ----
  wire        rup      = guard & (sticky | mant[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_rnd = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};
  wire        carry    = frac_rnd[10];
  wire [9:0]  frac_out = frac_rnd[9:0];

  // ---- result exponent ----
  wire [4:0] er5   = er[4:0];
  wire       ovf   = ~sub_path & ((er + {6'd0, carry}) >= 7'sd31);
  wire [4:0] e_y   = ovf ? 5'd31 : (sub_path ? {4'd0, carry} : (er5 + {4'd0, carry}));

  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result that rounded to zero
  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;
  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &
                  (any_zero | (sub_path & (frac_out == 10'd0) & ~carry));

  // ---- pack ----
  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 : is_inf ? 5'd31 : res_zero ? 5'd0 : e_y;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 : frac_out;
  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

  -> create_file(filename='v4_fastexp.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v4: pre-normalized subnormal inputs (product always normalized) + exponent\n// arithmetic hoisted in front of the multiplier:\n//   pre = Ea + Eb - 15      (before multiplier)\n//   t   = 11 - pre          (subnormal-path shift select, p21-independent)\n//   er  = pre + p21         (after multiplier, 1-bit increment)\n// The post-multiplier exponent path is pure muxing of precomputed values;\n// rounding carry feeds a 2:1 mux instead of an adder.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective exponents & everything derived before the multiply ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;\n\n  wire [4:0] t     = 5'd11 - pre[4:0];       // subnormal-path shift, 1..31\n  wire       t_big = (t > 5'd12);            // shift >= 13 gives zero fraction\n  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];\n  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);\n  // er >= 31 / er == 30 decodes (er = pre + p21)\n  wire [6:0] pre_p1 = pre[6:0] + 7'd1;\n  wire       pre_ge31 = ~pre[6] & (pre[6:0] >= 7'd31);\n  wire       pre_ge30 = ~pre[6] & (pre[6:0] >= 7'd30);\n  wire       pre_eq30 = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);\n  wire       pre_eq29 = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);\n  // candidate normal exponents (5-bit) for the carry/p21 muxes\n  wire [4:0] e00 = pre[4:0];\n  wire [4:0] e01 = pre[4:0] + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e10 = pre[4:0] + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e11 = pre[4:0] + 5'd2;\n\n  // ---- significand product (always normalized) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0\n  wire ge31  = pre_ge31 | (pre_eq30 & p21);    // er >= 31\n  wire eq30  = (pre_eq30 & ~p21) | (pre_eq29 & p21);\n\n  // ---- normal path: fraction fields straight from the product ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n  wire       rup      = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_n  = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry   = frac_n[10];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path: right shift prod by t (select known pre-multiply) ----\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (t)\n      5'd1: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end\n      5'd2: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end\n      5'd3: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end\n      5'd4: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end\n      5'd5: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end\n      5'd6: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end\n      5'd7: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end\n      5'd8: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end\n      5'd9: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end\n      5'd10:begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end\n      5'd11:begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end\n      5'd12:begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;            g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end\n    endcase\n  end\n  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | f_tr[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};\n  wire        carry_s = frac_s[10];\n\n  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----\n  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));\n  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? e01 : e00;      // er mod 32\n  wire [4:0] t2   = p21 ? e11 : e10;      // er + 1 mod 32\n  wire [4:0] e_n  = carry ? t2 : t1;\n\n  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result rounding to zero\n  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;\n  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &\n                  (any_zero | (under & ~carry_s & (frac_s[9:0] == 10'd0)));\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_inf  ? 5'd31 :\n                      res_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      under   ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_n;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      under   ? (carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_s[9:0]) :\n                                frac_n[9:0];\n  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

[Step 27/60]
  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});
  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});
  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;

  wire signed [6:0] t7 = 7'sd11 - pre;       // subnormal-path shift; p21 cancels!
  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];
  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);
  // er >= 31 / er == 30 decodes (er = pre + p21)
  wire [6:0] pre_p1 = pre[6:0] + 7'd1;
  wire       pre_ge31 = ~pre[6] & (pre[6:0] >= 7'd31);
  wire       pre_ge30 = ~pre[6] & (pre[6:0] >= 7'd30);
  wire       pre_eq30 = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);
  wire       pre_eq29 = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);
  // candidate normal exponents (5-bit) for the carry/p21 muxes
  wire [4:0] e00 = pre[4:0];
  wire [4:0] e01 = pre[4:0] + 5'd1;
  wire [4:0] e10 = pre[4:0] + 5'd1;
  wire [4:0] e11 = pre[4:0] + 5'd2;

  // ---- significand product (always normalized) ----
  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;
  wire        p21  = prod[21];

  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0
  wire ge31  = pre_ge31 | (pre_eq30 & p21);    // er >= 31
  wire eq30  = (pre_eq30 & ~p21) | (pre_eq29 & p21);

  // ---- normal path: fraction fields straight from the product ----
  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];
  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];
  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];
  wire       rup      = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_n  = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};
  wire        carry   = frac_n[10];

  // ---- subnormal path: right shift prod by t (select known pre-multiply) ----
  // on the underflow path t in [11, 29]; t>=23 rounds to zero (handled default)
  reg [9:0] f_tr;
  reg       g_s, st_s;
  always @* begin
    case (t7)
      7'sd11: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end
      7'sd12: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end
      7'sd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end
      7'sd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end
      7'sd15: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end
      7'sd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end
      7'sd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end
      7'sd18: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end
      7'sd19: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end
      7'sd20: begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end
      7'sd21: begin f_tr = {9'd0, prod[21]};    g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end
      7'sd22: begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end
      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;              g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end
    endcase
  end
  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | f_tr[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};
  wire        carry_s = frac_s[10];

  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----
  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));
  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? e01 : e00;      // er mod 32
  wire [4:0] t2   = p21 ? e11 : e10;      // er + 1 mod 32
  wire [4:0] e_n  = carry ? t2 : t1;

  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result rounding to zero
  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;
  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &
                  (any_zero | (under & ~carry_s & (frac_s[9:0] == 10'd0)));

  // ---- pack ----
  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 :
                      is_inf  ? 5'd31 :
                      res_zero ? 5'd0 :
                      under   ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_n;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 :
                      (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 :
                      under   ? (carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_s[9:0]) :
                                frac_n[9:0];
  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
v5 is slightly worse (112306) than v4 (111388), so I'll revert to v4 as the base. Key optimization for v6: make the rounding **carry** available without waiting for the incrementer (carry = rup & all-ones, a fast AND tree), so exponent/overflow logic doesn't depend on the incrementer.


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v6 = v4 + fast carry detection: carry_n = rup & &frac_raw (no incrementer in\n// the exponent/overflow path); incrementers only produce fraction bits.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective exponents & everything derived before the multiply ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t7 = 7'sd11 - pre;       // subnormal-path shift (p21 cancels)\n  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];\n  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);\n  wire [4:0] pre5    = pre[4:0];\n  wire       ge31_0  = ~pre[6] & (pre[5:0] >= 6'd31);\n  wire       eq30_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);\n  wire       eq29_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);\n  wire [4:0] e00 = pre5;\n  wire [4:0] e01 = pre5 + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e11 = pre5 + 5'd2;\n\n  // ---- significand product (always normalized) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0\n  wire ge31  = ge31_0 | (eq30_0 & p21);        // er >= 31\n  wire eq30  = (eq30_0 & ~p21) | (eq29_0 & p21);\n\n  // ---- normal path: fraction fields straight from the product ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n  wire       rup      = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_n  = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry   = rup & (&frac_raw);     // fast carry (no incrementer)\n\n  // ---- subnormal path: right shift prod by t7 ----\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (t7)\n      7'sd11: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end\n      7'sd12: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end\n      7'sd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end\n      7'sd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end\n      7'sd15: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end\n      7'sd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end\n      7'sd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end\n      7'sd18: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end\n      7'sd19: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end\n      7'sd20: begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end\n      7'sd21: begin f_tr = {9'd0, prod[21]};    g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end\n      7'sd22: begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;              g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end\n    endcase\n  end\n  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | f_tr[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};\n  wire        carry_s = rup_s & (&f_tr);       // fast carry (no incrementer)\n\n  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----\n  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));\n  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? e01 : e00;      // er mod 32\n  wire [4:0] t2   = p21 ? e11 : e01;      // er + 1 mod 32\n  wire [4:0] e_n  = carry ? t2 : t1;\n\n  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result rounding to zero\n  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;\n  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &\n                  (any_zero | (under & ~carry_s & (frac_s[9:0] == 10'd0)));\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_inf  ? 5'd31 :\n                      res_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      under   ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_n;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      under   ? (carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_s[9:0]) :\n                                frac_n[9:0];\n  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v6_fastcarry.v')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v7 = v4 + decomposed multiplier (ma=2^10+A, mb=2^10+B:\n//      prod = 2^20 + (A+B)*2^10 + A*B, 10x10 core instead of 11x11)\n//      + OR-form output packing (flat 2-level logic instead of mux chains).\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [9:0]  A = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t7 = 7'sd11 - pre;       // subnormal-path shift (p21 cancels)\n  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];\n  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);\n  wire [4:0] pre5    = pre[4:0];\n  wire       ge31_0  = ~pre[6] & (pre[5:0] >= 6'd31);\n  wire       eq30_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);\n  wire       eq29_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);\n  wire [4:0] e00 = pre5;\n  wire [4:0] e01 = pre5 + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e11 = pre5 + 5'd2;\n\n  // ---- significand product: (2^10+A)(2^10+B) = 2^20 + (A+B)2^10 + AB ----\n  wire [10:0] S    = A + B;\n  wire [19:0] P    = A * B;\n  wire [21:0] prod = {1'b0, S, 10'd0} + {2'b00, P} + 22'h100000;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0\n  wire ge31  = ge31_0 | (eq30_0 & p21);        // er >= 31\n  wire eq30  = (eq30_0 & ~p21) | (eq29_0 & p21);\n\n  // ---- normal path: fraction fields straight from the product ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n  wire       rup      = g_n & (s_n | frac_raw[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_n  = {1'b0, frac_raw} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry   = frac_n[10];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path: right shift prod by t7 ----\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (t7)\n      7'sd11: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end\n      7'sd12: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end\n      7'sd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end\n      7'sd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end\n      7'sd15: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end\n      7'sd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end\n      7'sd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end\n      7'sd18: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end\n      7'sd19: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end\n      7'sd20: begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end\n      7'sd21: begin f_tr = {9'd0, prod[21]};    g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end\n      7'sd22: begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;              g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end\n    endcase\n  end\n  wire        rup_s   = g_s & (st_s | f_tr[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_s  = {1'b0, f_tr} + {10'd0, rup_s};\n  wire        carry_s = frac_s[10];\n\n  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----\n  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));\n  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? [ASSISTANT]
v7 (117550) is worse than v4 (111388). Let me build v8 which unifies the two rounding incrementers into one and uses fast carry (`carry = (&mant) & g`) to remove the incrementer from the exponent/overflow critical path:


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v8 = v4 + unified single rounding incrementer (normal & subnormal share it via\n//      mant mux) + fast carry carry=(&mant)&g removed from the incrementer.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective exponents & everything derived before the multiply ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t7 = 7'sd11 - pre;       // subnormal-path shift (p21 cancels)\n  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];\n  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);\n  wire [4:0] pre5    = pre[4:0];\n  wire       ge31_0  = ~pre[6] & (pre[5:0] >= 6'd31);\n  wire       eq30_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);\n  wire       eq29_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);\n  wire [4:0] e00 = pre5;\n  wire [4:0] e01 = pre5 + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e11 = pre5 + 5'd2;\n\n  // ---- significand product (always normalized) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0\n  wire ge31  = ge31_0 | (eq30_0 & p21);        // er >= 31\n  wire eq30  = (eq30_0 & ~p21) | (eq29_0 & p21);\n\n  // ---- normal path mantissa fields ----\n  wire [9:0] frac_raw = p21 ? prod[20:11] : prod[19:10];\n  wire       g_n      = p21 ? prod[10]    : prod[9];\n  wire       s_n      = p21 ? |prod[9:0]  : |prod[8:0];\n\n  // ---- subnormal path: right shift prod by t7 ----\n  reg [9:0] f_tr;\n  reg       g_s, st_s;\n  always @* begin\n    case (t7)\n      7'sd11: begin f_tr = prod[20:11];         g_s = prod[10]; st_s = |prod[9:0];  end\n      7'sd12: begin f_tr = prod[21:12];         g_s = prod[11]; st_s = |prod[10:0]; end\n      7'sd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0]; end\n      7'sd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; st_s = |prod[12:0]; end\n      7'sd15: begin f_tr = {3'd0, prod[21:15]}; g_s = prod[14]; st_s = |prod[13:0]; end\n      7'sd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0]; end\n      7'sd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16]; st_s = |prod[15:0]; end\n      7'sd18: begin f_tr = {6'd0, prod[21:18]}; g_s = prod[17]; st_s = |prod[16:0]; end\n      7'sd19: begin f_tr = {7'd0, prod[21:19]}; g_s = prod[18]; st_s = |prod[17:0]; end\n      7'sd20: begin f_tr = {8'd0, prod[21:20]}; g_s = prod[19]; st_s = |prod[18:0]; end\n      7'sd21: begin f_tr = {9'd0, prod[21]};    g_s = prod[20]; st_s = |prod[19:0]; end\n      7'sd22: begin f_tr = 10'd0;               g_s = prod[21]; st_s = |prod[20:0]; end\n      default: begin f_tr = 10'd0;              g_s = 1'b0;     st_s = 1'b0;        end\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  // ---- unified rounding: select mantissa, single incrementer, fast carry ----\n  wire [9:0] mant = under ? f_tr : frac_raw;\n  wire       g    = under ? g_s   : g_n;\n  wire       st   = under ? st_s  : s_n;\n  wire       rup  = g & (st | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_rnd = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire       carry = (&mant) & g;              // fast carry (no incrementer)\n  wire [9:0] frac_out  = frac_rnd[9:0];\n\n  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----\n  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));\n  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? e01 : e00;      // er mod 32\n  wire [4:0] t2   = p21 ? e11 : e01;      // er + 1 mod 32\n  wire [4:0] e_n  = carry ? t2 : t1;\n\n  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result rounding to zero\n  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;\n  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &\n                  (any_zero | (under & ~carry & (frac_out == 10'd0)));\n\n  // ---- pack ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_inf  ? 5'd31 :\n                      res_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      under   ? {4'd0, carry} : e_n;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      under   ? (carry ? 10'd0 : frac_out) :\n                                frac_out;\n  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v8_unified_round.v')

v8 is the new best (110816). The `target_delay` change had no effect on the netlist. Let me analyze the critical path — it runs through classification → normalization shift → multiplier → subnormal shift mux → rounding → packing. 

Let me try v9: replace the 13-case subnormal shift mux with a unified 4-stage barrel shifter (both normal and subnormal paths share it), and simplify packing (since `frac_out` is already 0 when subnormal+carry).


  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v9 = v8 with a unified 4-stage barrel shifter for mantissa extraction\n//      (normal & subnormal share one shift), plus simplified packing\n//      (frac_out is already 0 on subnormal+carry, so no extra mux).\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fa[i]) lza = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n  always @* begin\n    lzb = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; i < 10; i = i + 1) if (fb[i]) lzb = 4'd9 - i[3:0];\n  end\n\n  // ---- normalized significands ----\n  wire [10:0] fa_sh = {1'b0, fa} << (lza + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] fb_sh = {1'b0, fb} << (lzb + 4'd1);\n  wire [10:0] ma = ea_zero ? {1'b1, fa_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fa};\n  wire [10:0] mb = eb_zero ? {1'b1, fb_sh[9:0]} : {1'b1, fb};\n\n  // ---- effective exponents & everything derived before the multiply ----\n  wire signed [6:0] Ea = ea_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lza}) : $signed({2'd0, ea});\n  wire signed [6:0] Eb = eb_zero ? -$signed({3'd0, lzb}) : $signed({2'd0, eb});\n  wire signed [6:0] pre = Ea + Eb - 7'sd15;\n\n  wire signed [6:0] t7 = 7'sd11 - pre;       // subnormal-path shift (p21 cancels)\n  wire       pre_neg = pre[6];\n  wire       pre_eq0 = (pre[5:0] == 6'd0);\n  wire [4:0] pre5    = pre[4:0];\n  wire       ge31_0  = ~pre[6] & (pre[5:0] >= 6'd31);\n  wire       eq30_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd30);\n  wire       eq29_0  = (pre[6:0] == 7'd29);\n  wire [4:0] e00 = pre5;\n  wire [4:0] e01 = pre5 + 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] e11 = pre5 + 5'd2;\n\n  // ---- significand product (always normalized) ----\n  wire [21:0] prod = ma * mb;\n  wire        p21  = prod[21];\n\n  wire under = pre_neg | (pre_eq0 & ~p21);     // er <= 0\n  wire ge31  = ge31_0 | (eq30_0 & p21);        // er >= 31\n  wire eq30  = (eq30_0 & ~p21) | (eq29_0 & p21);\n\n  // ---- unified mantissa extraction via one barrel shift ----\n  // P = prod >> u ; mant=P[19:10], guard=P[9], sticky=|P[8:0]\n  //   normal   : u = p21          (aligns leading 1 to bit 20 of P)\n  //   subnormal: u = t7 - 10       (aligns onto the subnormal grid)\n  wire        too_small = under & (t7 >= 7'sd23);\n  wire [3:0]  u  = under ? (t7[3:0] - 4'd10) : {3'd0, p21};\n  wire [21:0] P  = prod >> u;\n  wire [9:0]  mant = too_small ? 10'd0 : P[19:10];\n  wire        g    = too_small ? 1'b0  : P[9];\n  wire        st   = too_small ? 1'b0  : |P[8:0];\n\n  // ---- single rounding incrementer + fast carry ----\n  wire        rup  = g & (st | mant[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_rnd = {1'b0, mant} + {10'd0, rup};\n  wire        carry = (&mant) & g;              // fast carry (no incrementer)\n  wire [9:0]  frac_out = frac_rnd[9:0];\n\n  // ---- result exponent & overflow ----\n  wire       ovf  = ~under & (ge31 | (eq30 & carry));\n  wire [4:0] t1   = p21 ? e01 : e00;      // er mod 32\n  wire [4:0] t2   = p21 ? e11 : e01;      // er + 1 mod 32\n  wire [4:0] e_n  = carry ? t2 : t1;\n\n  // signed zero: zero input, or subnormal-path result rounding to zero\n  wire is_inf   = any_inf | ovf;\n  wire res_zero = ~any_nan & ~is_inf &\n                  (any_zero | (under & ~carry & (frac_out == 10'd0)));\n\n  // ---- pack (frac_out is already 0 when subnormal+carry) ----\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = any_nan ? 5'd31 :\n                      is_inf  ? 5'd31 :\n                      res_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      under   ? {4'd0, carry} : e_n;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = any_nan ? 10'h200 :\n                      (is_inf | res_zero) ? 10'd0 : frac_out;\n  wire sign_y = any_nan ? 1'b0 : sign_p;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='v9_barrel.v')

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- binary16 FP multiplier.\n// v10 = v8 + fast zero-detect (res_zero computed from f_tr/rup_s without the\n// incrementer) + removed redundant carry-mux from fraction packing\n// (frac_out[9:0] is already 0 when carry=1).\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // ---- unpack & classify ----\n  wire        sa = a[15], sb = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  ea = a[14:10], eb = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  fa = a[9:0],   fb = b[9:0];\n\n  wire ea_zero = (ea == 5'd0),  eb_zero = (eb == 5'd0);\n  wire ea_ones = &ea,           eb_ones = &eb;\n  wire fa_zero = (fa == 10'd0), fb_zero = (fb == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_a  = ea_zero & fa_zero,  zero_b = eb_zero & fb_zero;\n  wire nan_a   = ea_ones & ~fa_zero, nan_b  = eb_ones & ~fb_zero;\n  wire inf_a   = ea_ones & fa_zero,  inf_b  = eb_ones & fb_zero;\n\n  wire any_nan  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire any_inf  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire any_zero = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sign_p   = sa ^ sb;\n\n  // ---- leading-zero count of the 10-bit fractions ----\n  reg [3:0] lza, lzb;\n  integer i;\n  always @* begin\n    lza = 4'd9;\n    for (i = 0; end\n      7'sd13: begin f_tr = {1'b0, prod[21:13]}; g_s = prod[12]; st_s = |prod[11:0];\n                     f_tr_zero = ~|prod[21:13]; end\n      7'sd14: begin f_tr = {2'd0, prod[21:14]}; g_s = prod[13]; end\n      7'sd16: begin f_tr = {4'd0, prod[21:16]}; g_s = prod[15]; st_s = |prod[14:0];\n                     f_tr_zero = ~|prod[21:16]; end\n      7'sd17: begin f_tr = {5'd0, prod[21:17]}; g_s = prod[16];
